## Suspected conflicts

> [!warning] Conflict
> Flagged while digesting this document, not verified: The same app is assigned different business roles across scope items: Execute Billing - Mass Run (F3525) and Execute Invoicing - Mass Run (F3524) use SAP_BR_ADMINISTRATOR in 2BE but SAP_BR_INVOICING_ADMIN_CINV in 2BG; Print Correspondence (F3537) uses SAP_BR_ADMIN_APR_FICA, SAP_BR_ADMINISTRATOR or SAP_BR_INVOICING_ADMIN_CINV depending on scope item.
